## Cross-Verification Guide

To verify fixes, check the following:

1. **Templating:** Search for the structural pattern in the question stem. If the same pattern (with numbers replaced by N) appears more than 2 times, it is still templated.
2. **Short Explanations:** Check that all explanations are at least 40 characters and provide pedagogical context (not just formula substitution).
3. **Vacuous Explanations:** Check that explanations do not start with 'the correct answer is', 'option', 'as given', 'as per', or 'by definition' without additional context.
4. **Very Short Questions:** Check that all questions are at least 40 characters and test reasoning, not just recall.
5. **Missing KaTeX:** Check that all mathematical notation uses KaTeX format (\( ... \) for inline, \[ ... \] for block).
